Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hydrocarbons variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 28 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 270 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hydrocarbons generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 270. QED.
